964NoteDECISION ANALYSIS (post m142). The simulator's magnetic model produces NO negative lobes (all-positive then zero), but real data has strong negatives (trough -45 at x150, -179 at x-165). So simulator cannot fit magnetic SHAPE faithfully — I fit only the positive-lobe breadth. Positive lobe: flat to x~50, half-max(50) at r~74, zero-crossing at r~92 (right)/-92(left). This requires laterally EXTENDED body: half-width(size)~80, and with top~25m (sp20=98≈bg, sp40=75) => center depth ~105-110. Compact deep body (size55) tested: x60=48 vs meas 87 → rejected. So size~80 REQUIRED (prior submits used 60-70 → too small, likely the error). Resistivity: DC sounding (x0: 98,90,75,64,56,51,45,39 sp20..110) fit best by res~12 (res 1-4 was 2nd error). Susceptibility: peak 101-105nT; sim size84/d112/res12/susc0.0095→117 → susc~0.0082-0.0088. Center x: DC min x=0-8 (m138-140: -5=38.99,2=40.09,8=37.76), mag peak x10-25 → x~5. FINAL: x=5, depth=110, size=80, res=12, susc=0.0085.
